# TrustCopilot backlog (Jira snapshot)

Snapshot of Jira project SCRUM taken 2026-11-05: 546 issues (15 done). **Jira is the source of truth** for status, due dates and sprints: <https://reinhartjm294trustcopilot.atlassian.net/jira/software/projects/SCRUM/boards/1>. This file exists so agents without Jira access can see the full plan.

- Original scope (TC-01..TC-30) keeps its full spec in `docs/planning/tickets/`; the Spec column links there.
- Post-freeze epics E31–E36 (scope-freeze §11), TC-17-003a and SCRUM-35 (how to work tickets) have their Jira text in `docs/planning/jira/tickets/`.
- E37 (the PDF engine, a separate product) lives in the pdf-engine repo: <https://github.com/Johnathan-u/pdf-engine/blob/main/docs/backlog/backlog.md>.
- Acceptance state comes from `docs/planning/backlog-state.json`, not from Jira status; the Status column shows both. Dependencies combine Jira links and each ticket's `depends_on:` line; a dependency counts as cleared when Jira or the tracker says DONE. `tracker-blocked` repeats a blocker recorded in the tracker.
- Flags: `agent-ready` = an agent may implement; `human-only` / `owner-decision` / `owner-task` = the owner's; `needs-owner-accounts` = needs an account or credential from the owner; `needs-your-review` = the owner reviews before it closes.
- Machine-readable copy: `backlog.json` (same data, without descriptions).
- Jira status **Idea** is this board's not-started column, not unapproved scope (E31–E37 are approved under OD-11). Neither Jira Idea nor Jira Done means acceptance; acceptance is recorded only in TrustCopilot's `docs/planning/backlog-state.json`.
- "Ready" means unblocked, not "start now". Work in due-date order, and start a ticket only when it is assigned to you or its Jira start date has arrived (OD-11). Release order: v1.0 (Dec 15, 2026), then v1.1 (E32 and the E37 integration), then v2.0 (E34); see `docs/acceptance/scope-freeze.md` §11. E32 and E34 points are not estimated yet.
